([GitFA96/projectLC](https://github.com/GitFA96/projectLC/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/chain-reviewer.md))
- **chain-step-reviewer**: Review ONE completed autonomous_safe chain step's output against that step's own declared acceptance criteria and return a structured verdict. - **chain-tracker**: Persistent attack chain state tracker across sessions. Maintains a graph of findings, potential chains, and verified multi-hop paths in a target's Attack Chains directory. Different from attack-chain-deep-dive (single-session analysis) — this agent persists and evolves chain state. Use when user says 'update chain graph', 'track chain', 'chain status', 'what chains are possible', 'attack path update'. ([guan4tou2/bug-bounty-vault-framework](https://github.com/guan4tou2/bug-bounty-vault-framework/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/chain-tracker.md) · ★2)
- **chain-verifier**: Use this agent when verifying that a completed multi-phase pipeline satisfied its original requirements. Typical triggers include finishing all implementation phases and needing a final integrity check, reaching the mandatory chain-verifier step in the planned pipeline shape, and confirming APPROVED status before closing a pipeline. Does not receive chain history — fresh context is required. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([MinhThang1009/dotclaude](https://github.com/MinhThang1009/dotclaude/blob/HEAD/plugins/subagent-system/agents/chain-verifier.md) · ★20)
- **chair-advocate**: The advocate chair in the docs-assist authoring loop. Shapes a cold packet into a document and declares, as a ledger, everything it added that the packet did not contain. Use for the reader-facing half of a loop run, whether the cast is a technical writer or an instructional designer. ([EdwardAngert/docs-agent-plugin](https://github.com/EdwardAngert/docs-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/chair-advocate.md) · ★5)
- **chair-authority**: The authority chair in the docs-assist authoring loop. Emits a cold, prose-free packet of what is true about a topic, then reviews ledger entries the advocate chair added. Use for the subject-matter half of a loop run, whether the cast is a practitioner or a domain expert. ([EdwardAngert/docs-agent-plugin](https://github.com/EdwardAngert/docs-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/chair-authority.md) · ★5)
- **chair-continuity**: The continuity chair in the docs-assist authoring loop. Reconciles a packet's example values, naming, and sequencing against the rest of the documentation set so a reader working straight through meets something coherent. Also runs at the preparation gate over a finished set. ([EdwardAngert/docs-agent-plugin](https://github.com/EdwardAngert/docs-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/chair-continuity.md) · ★5)

- **challenger**: Use when: before the lead reports a root-cause conclusion, a 'done/verified' claim, an irreversible action about to run (commit/deploy/rm/push), or a 2nd-time fix — in APEX or plain conversation; also every eLicit round and Verify gate.
